# 039 — every card of 2026-10-04, and the rule behind each of its values

Measured 2026-10-04 by the scratchpad migrator of M-issue-files, over the tree
at `99a67630` moved into `issues/` (`.claude/rules/records.md` § The issues).
One row per issue file: its path, then the rule that produced its `kind`, its
`filed`, its `commit` and its `milestone`. The values are the files' own cards;
this table is their provenance, so any value can be traced to a rule and the
rule to a count. Two kinds were corrected after the first `records` run read
two records ABOUT a defect's decision as the defect's own (rows marked *corrected*).

**kind**, by rule:

- 759 — the log is the register of decisions
- 436 — the learning list
- 270 — 1 defect shape
- 264 — 2 a sitting or a ratification
- 97 — 4 a verdict word
- 78 — the defect list
- 67 — 3 a milestone item, read one by one
- 54 — 6 read one by one
- 51 — read one by one
- 25 — 5 a comprehension question
- 1 — the decision list

**filed**, by rule:

- 1216 — the stamp
- 462 — the earliest day its line states
- 381 — the Origin day
- 39 — the settling commit, earlier than the stamp
- 3 — the day of the commit that filed it, its Origin naming none
- 1 — the day of the earliest commit that wrote its line, its Origin naming none

**commit**, by rule:

- 971 — history: earliest commit that added it
- 436 — a question is not settled by a commit
- 339 — the commit that wrote it into the record
- 123 — history: earliest commit that added it, read past a folded first field
- 91 — Repaired at
- 73 — open, not repaired
- 52 — open
- 16 — history: earliest commit that added it, a move on the entry's own day
- 1 — history: only a move added it

**milestone**, by rule:

- 738 — no milestone in the first field
- 658 — a log row names no milestone field
- 436 — its first field
- 111 — first field names a chain row
- 78 — a defect is a batch's, not a milestone's
- 52 — the page it stood on
- 10 — old id via the chain tag m1
- 8 — old id via the chain tag m2
- 4 — old id via the chain tag m6
- 3 — old id via the chain tag m0
- 1 — old id via the chain tag m4
- 1 — old id via the chain tag m5a
- 1 — old id via the chain tag m5c
- 1 — old id via the chain tag m8a
